+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                         \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
+        }                                                                        \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
+// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
+// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
+// +column, -column.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
+                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
+                                     int n_elems_root) {
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
+    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
+    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
+    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
+        total_flux = 0.5;
+    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
+               (row == last && column == 0)) {
+        total_flux = -0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
+    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
+    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (row > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+
+    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
+    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
 }
 
+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};
+

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                         \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \

+// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
+// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
+// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
+// +column, -column.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
+                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
+                                     int n_elems_root) {
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
+    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
+    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
+    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
+        total_flux = 0.5;
+    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
+               (row == last && column == 0)) {
+        total_flux = -0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
+    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
+    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (row > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+
+    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
+    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
 }
 
+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                         \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
+        }                                                                        \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
+// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
+// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
+// +column, -column.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
+                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
+                                     int n_elems_root) {
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
+    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
+    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
+    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
+        total_flux = 0.5;
+    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
+               (row == last && column == 0)) {
+        total_flux = -0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
+    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
+    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (row > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+
+    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
+    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
 }
 
+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};

-void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                         \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
+        }                                                                        \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
+// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
+// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
+// +column, -column.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
+                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
+                                     int n_elems_root) {
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
+    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
+    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
+    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
+        total_flux = 0.5;
+    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
+               (row == last && column == 0)) {
+        total_flux = -0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
+    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
+    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (row > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+
+    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
+    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
 }
 
+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};
+

 bool validateResults(const World& world) {
     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                         \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
+        }                                                                        \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
+// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
+// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
+// +column, -column.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
+                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
+                                     int n_elems_root) {
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
+    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
+    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
+    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
+        total_flux = 0.5;
+    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
+               (row == last && column == 0)) {
+        total_flux = -0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
+    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
+    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (row > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+
+    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
+    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
 }
 
+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                         \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
+        }                                                                        \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
+// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
+// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
+// +column, -column.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
+                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
+                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
+                                     int n_elems_root) {
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
+    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
+    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
+    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
+        total_flux = 0.5;
+    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
+               (row == last && column == 0)) {
+        total_flux = -0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
+    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
+    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (row > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
+                                          current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+    if (column > 0) {
+        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
+        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
+        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
+    }
+
+    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
+    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
 }
 
+// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
+// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
+// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
+          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
+        }
+
+        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+
+        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
+        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
+                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
+
+        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
+        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
+            captureGraph();
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
+        }
+        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+        }
+        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
+            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
+        }
+        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void run() {
+        int completed = 0;
+        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
+               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
+            completed += graph_iterations_;
+        }
+
+        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
+        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
+            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) {
+        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
+            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
+            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+private:
+    void captureGraph() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
+            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
+                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
+                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
+    int n_iters_ = 0;
+    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
+    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    dim3 block_{};
+    dim3 grid_{};
+};
+

     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

    15	// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements
    16	using idx_t = uint64_t;
    17	using val_t = double;
    18	
    19	// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)
    20	constexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;
    21	
    22	// Material properties for energy transfer
    23	struct Material {
    24	    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient
    25	    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink
    26	};
    27	
    28	// Static connectivity information for each element
    29	struct ElementStatic {
    30	    idx_t material_idx;
    31	    idx_t num_connections;
    32	    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements
    33	    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection
    34	};
    35	
    36	// Dynamic state for each element
    37	struct ElementDynamic {
    38	    val_t current_energy;
    39	    val_t total_flux;
    40	};
    41	
    42	// World state
    43	struct World {
    44	    std::vector<Material> materials;
    45	    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;
    46	    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;
    47	    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;
    48	};
    49	
    50	// Material type IDs
    51	constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
    52	constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
    53	constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;
    54	
    55	// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
    56	// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
    57	void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {
    58	    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
    59	    
    60	    // Initialize materials
    61	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material
    62	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material
    63	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material
    64	    
    65	    // Allocate elements
    66	    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);
    67	    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);
    68	    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);
    69	    
    70	    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy
    71	    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
    72	        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
    73	        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;
    74	        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;
    75	        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;
    76	    }
    77	    
    78	    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid
    79	    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {
    80	        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {
    81	            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;
    82	            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];
    83	            
    84	            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)
    85	            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
    86	            
    87	            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {
    88	                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];
    89	                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];
    90	                
    91	                // Check if neighbor is within bounds
    92	                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {
    93	                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;
    94	                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;
    95	                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;
    96	                    elem.num_connections++;
    97	                }
    98	            }
    99	        }
   100	    }
   101	    
   102	    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
   103	    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
   104	    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
   105	    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
   106	    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
   107	    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
   108	}
   109	
   110	// Compute energy flux between two elements
   111	inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
   112	                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
   113	    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
   114	           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
   115	}
   116	
   117	[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
   118	                              const char* file, int line) {
   119	    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
   120	                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
   121	    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   122	}
   123	
   124	#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
   125	    do {                                                                         \
   126	        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
   127	        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
   128	            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
   129	        }                                                                        \
   130	    } while (false)
   131	
   132	// Each CUDA thread owns one mesh element.  The x dimension follows the
   133	// contiguous grid coordinate so every warp performs coalesced accesses.  The
   134	// connection order is exactly the one produced by buildSquare2D: +row, -row,
   135	// +column, -column.
   136	__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,
   137	                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,
   138	                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,
   139	                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,
   140	                                     int n_elems_root) {
   141	    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x);
   142	    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y);
   143	    if (row >= n_elems_root || column >= n_elems_root) {
   144	        return;
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(row) * n_elems_root + column;
   148	    const val_t current_energy = energy_read[index];
   149	    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
   150	
   151	    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
   152	    if ((row == 0 && column == 0) || (row == last && column == last)) {
   153	        total_flux = 0.5;
   154	    } else if ((row == 0 && column == last) ||
   155	               (row == last && column == 0)) {
   156	        total_flux = -0.5;
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    // Explicit round-to-nearest operations prevent contraction or reassociation
   160	    // from changing the reference benchmark's bitwise results.
   161	    if (row + 1 < n_elems_root) {
   162	        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + n_elems_root],
   163	                                          current_energy);
   164	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
   165	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
   166	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
   167	        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
   168	    }
   169	    if (row > 0) {
   170	        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - n_elems_root],
   171	                                          current_energy);
   172	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
   173	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
   174	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
   175	        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
   176	    }
   177	    if (column + 1 < n_elems_root) {
   178	        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index + 1], current_energy);
   179	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
   180	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
   181	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
   182	        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
   183	    }
   184	    if (column > 0) {
   185	        val_t connection_flux = __dsub_rn(energy_read[index - 1], current_energy);
   186	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.8);
   187	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 1.0);
   188	        connection_flux = __dmul_rn(connection_flux, 0.25);
   189	        total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, connection_flux);
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    energy_write[index] = __dadd_rn(current_energy, total_flux);
   193	    flux_write[index] = __dadd_rn(flux_read[index], fabs(total_flux));
   194	}
   195	
   196	// Owns the GPU representation of the dynamic mesh state.  A graph contains up
   197	// to 64 dependent iterations; long simulations replay that graph in chunks to
   198	// keep both launch overhead and graph construction/storage bounded.
   199	class CudaSimulation {
   200	public:
   201	    CudaSimulation(const World& world, int n_elems_root, int n_iters)
   202	        : n_elems_(world.elements_dynamic.size()),
   203	          n_elems_root_(n_elems_root), n_iters_(std::max(n_iters, 0)) {
   204	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   205	
   206	        const size_t bytes = n_elems_ * sizeof(val_t);
   207	        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
   208	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&energy_[buffer]), bytes));
   209	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&flux_[buffer]), bytes));
   210	        }
   211	
   212	        // Convert the host array-of-structures to coalesced device arrays.
   213	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
   214	            energy_[0], sizeof(val_t),
   215	            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
   216	            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
   217	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
   218	            flux_[0], sizeof(val_t),
   219	            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
   220	            sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
   221	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
   222	
   223	        block_ = dim3(32, 8);
   224	        grid_ = dim3((n_elems_root_ + block_.x - 1) / block_.x,
   225	                     (n_elems_root_ + block_.y - 1) / block_.y);
   226	
   227	        graph_iterations_ = std::min(n_iters_, 64);
   228	        if (graph_iterations_ > 0) {
   229	            captureGraph();
   230	        }
   231	    }
   232	
   233	    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
   234	    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
   235	
   236	    ~CudaSimulation() {
   237	        if (graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
   238	            cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_exec_);
   239	        }
   240	        if (graph_ != nullptr) {
   241	            cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
   242	        }
   243	        for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
   244	            cudaFree(energy_[buffer]);
   245	            cudaFree(flux_[buffer]);
   246	        }
   247	        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
   248	            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
   249	        }
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    void run() {
   253	        int completed = 0;
   254	        while (completed + graph_iterations_ <= n_iters_ &&
   255	               graph_iterations_ != 0) {
   256	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_exec_, stream_));
   257	            completed += graph_iterations_;
   258	        }
   259	
   260	        // This path is at most 63 launches and is empty for the usual case.
   261	        for (; completed < n_iters_; ++completed) {
   262	            const int read_buffer = completed & 1;
   263	            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
   264	            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
   265	                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
   266	                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
   267	        }
   268	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   269	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
   270	    }
   271	
   272	    void download(World& world) {
   273	        const int final_buffer = n_iters_ & 1;
   274	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
   275	            &world.elements_dynamic.front().current_energy, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
   276	            energy_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
   277	            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
   278	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(
   279	            &world.elements_dynamic.front().total_flux, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
   280	            flux_[final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), n_elems_,
   281	            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
   282	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
   283	    }
   284	
   285	private:
   286	    void captureGraph() {
   287	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
   288	        for (int iter = 0; iter < graph_iterations_; ++iter) {
   289	            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
   290	            const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
   291	            updateElementsKernel<<<grid_, block_, 0, stream_>>>(
   292	                energy_[read_buffer], flux_[read_buffer], energy_[write_buffer],
   293	                flux_[write_buffer], n_elems_root_);
   294	        }
   295	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph_));
   296	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   297	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    size_t n_elems_ = 0;
   301	    int n_elems_root_ = 0;
   302	    int n_iters_ = 0;
   303	    int graph_iterations_ = 0;
   304	    val_t* energy_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
   305	    val_t* flux_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
   306	    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
   307	    cudaGraph_t graph_ = nullptr;
   308	    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec_ = nullptr;
   309	    dim3 block_{};
   310	    dim3 grid_{};
   311	};
   312	
   313	// Validate simulation results
   314	bool validateResults(const World& world) {
   315	    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
   316	    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
   317	    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   318	    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   319	    
   320	    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
   321	        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
   322	        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
   323	        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
   324	        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);
   325	    }
   326	    
   327	    printf("Validation results:\n");
   328	    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
   329	    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
   330	    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
   331	    
   332	    // Check for numerical issues
   333	    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
   334	    
   335	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   336	        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
   337	        return false;
   338	    }
   339	    
   340	    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
   341	        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
   342	        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows
   343	    }
   344	    
   345	    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
   346	        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
   347	        return false;
   348	    }
   349	    
   350	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
   351	        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
   352	        return false;
   353	    }
   354	    
   355	    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   356	    return true;
   357	}

   360	uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {

-// Run simulation for n_iters iterations
-void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
